---
title: Item classes
description: Every item class the game ships, by category, with the name and GUID that giveItem, takeItem and GroundItem.spawn take.
sidebar:
  label: Items
  order: 116
---

[`player.giveItem`](../../reference/server/classes/Player.md#giveitem),
[`player.takeItem`](../../reference/server/classes/Player.md#takeitem) and
[`GroundItem.spawn`](../../reference/server/classes/GroundItem.md#spawn) take an item class
by its exact name in the game's tables or by its GUID. `Npc.create({ wearing })` takes GUIDs only.

```ts
player.giveItem("money", 500);
player.giveItem("alchemyOil", 3);
GroundItem.spawn("alchemyOil", player.position);
```

Names are case-sensitive. There is no runtime list: a name that is not here makes `giveItem`
return `false`. See [Items](../../players/items/) for equipment, taking items back and trades.
